- **team-os**: Install the BenAI Relay fork in an Obsidian vault as the foundation for a shared Team OS — replaces the official Relay (`system3-relay`) plugin with the BenAI fork (`benai-relay-fork`) which ships custom RBAC + access controls for team-wide vault sharing. Bundled production build ships inside the skill — user just points to their Obsidian vault path.
